FROM (
    SELECT r.id, r.child_id, r.created_at, r.expires_at, r.revoked_at, r.token_hash, r.user_id, u.id AS id0, u.created_at AS created_at0, u.email, u.full_name, u.is_active, u.password_hash, u.phone, u.role, u.updated_at

) AS s
